# Kenpachi: the sim-verified catalogue (PLAN4 kit study, step 2, 2026-11-15)

`python3 tools/combo-trial.py docs/research/strategy-drsi/sp/kits/kenpachi/trials.lisp` (main c15f9a55, `tools/simgate/trial.lisp`
unchanged). **1608 trials, all played in the native sim**; `trials.lisp` is generated by `gen.py` (one function per section below). The
tool's full table is at the end. The section tables are printed by `digest.py`, which plays the same file and keeps each trial's combat-log
events (first real hit, cancels, breakthroughs, Kikon, move starts): `python3 docs/research/strategy-drsi/sp/kits/kenpachi/digest.py`.
**Order check**: the 1548 scripted trials run backwards give the same end line (dmg, hits, gaps, adv, dist, FS, Konpaku, end state), the
same first hit and the same event sequence for every trial. Only the display ticks move by 1 now and then (the cancel's slow motion keeps a
fractional clock across trials). The 60 HARD-CPU trials (section Z) are **last in the file**: a CPU trial leaves brain and kit state
(Hisagi's KIN, the CPU's rolls) that `:seed` does not reset, so they depend on what ran before them, and a scripted trial right after one
can differ (seen once, at a boundary frame).

Setup: P1 Kenpachi presses only its script; P2 Ichigo or Hisagi, a standing dummy unless `:p2-at` (his J / SP on a **sim** frame) or
`:p2-press` (a button on a **display tick**: hitstop and the cancel's slow motion stretch ticks) drive him. Both flash-step gauges full,
9 Konpaku, no SUBLIMATION / DRIVE. Tags: **I** Ichigo's Shikai (takes x0.8), **IK** KESSA, **H** Hisagi's Shikai, **Hr** his Bankai
ranged mode, **Hb** his Bankai bind mode (it reverts to ranged after ~30-60 ticks of its own). "@" numbers in the tables are display ticks
from the trial's first press.

Corrections to the 2026-11-06 catalogue (`docs/research/ki-ai/kenpachi/catalogue.md`), from this run:
- **Every trial needs `:fs 300`**: `trial.lisp`'s P1 starts at 100 by default, so multi-segment routes paid short (DUEL_SP's damage
  scaling) or were refused. The old route numbers happen to agree (they predate the SP system); new ones must set it.
- **The sealed J1 cancels Ichigo's wave like the Shikai's** (projectiles joined the triangle, DUEL_SP §6): F31's "no cut" is refuted (G).
- **The sealed J1 is S9** since DUEL_KEN_V3 §48 (was 6): it no longer beats KESSA's J1 (S8) to the hit (T, IK row); it still beats Ichigo's
  and Hisagi's (S14).
- **A non-red victim cancels a Kikon follow-up with any attack** (C): Kikon value exists only against red.
- **A whiff's real recovery is the sheet's S + A + whiff R plus 10 frames** (F, both opponents, every move tried; mine the same: S). A J
  pressed inside one's own recovery is eaten by the move's latch, not buffered: the next move starts only from a press on the free frame.
- Two `trial.lisp` traps for anyone writing trials: P1's `:fs` defaults to 100 (set `:fs 300`), and `:p2-press` counts display ticks while
  `:p2-at` / `:wait` count sim frames (hitstop and the cancel's slow motion separate them).

## Findings

**Reach (centre to centre, 0.1 m; P / E / Q)**

| Band | Kenpachi reaches with | He reaches Kenpachi with |
|---|---|---|
| to 2.0-2.4 m | sealed J1 (S9) 2.6, KYORAN J1 (S7) 2.8, ZANKAI J1 2.7, Shikai J1 3.4 | Ichigo J1 (S14) 2.4, KESSA J1 (S8) 2.0, Hisagi J1 (S14) 2.4, bind J1 2.2 |
| 2.4-3.4 m | sealed NS1a (S14, slide 1.2) **3.3**, NS2a 3.0, Shikai J1 3.4 | Ichigo NS1a (S22) 3.1, KESSA NS1a 3.4, Hisagi NS2a (S16) 3.8, ranged NS1a (S14, 360°) 3.4 |
| 3.4-5.7 m | sealed tame > thrust 4.0 (S14 + 12), KYORAN lunge (S14) 4.9, **Shikai lunge (S16) 5.7** | Ichigo NS3a rush (S18) 5.1, Hisagi NS3a bind (S18) 5.2, ranged NS2a (S30) 2.5-4.9 |
| 3.0-6.7 m | — (nothing sealed past 4.0) | **Hisagi NS1a (S20) 3.0-6.4 and ranged J1 (S14) 3.0-6.4, dead under 3.0 m**; bind NS1a (S18) 1.5-6.7 |
| 3-8 m+ | — | Ichigo's / KESSA's Getsuga route (charge, charge, wave): 82 / 78 Reishi, the wave hits 60-81 ticks after its start |

**Routes (R)**: sealed J1-J5 AS3 171 / 215 (I / H), knockdown +56, 75 SP; NS1 a b c SA J2-J5 AS3 **214 / 269**; NS2 a b SA J2 J3 AS2
173 / 218 (launch +77). Shikai: L0 lunge 28 / 35 from up to 5.7 m; L2 ptr 2 NS a-d SA J2 J3 AS2 **251 / 315** (+2, ends L3); L3 J1-J3 AS2
(4 seg) 221 / 276 (+47); L3 special AS 166-198 / 208-249; the Bankai entry 145 / 182 (+77 at 6.1 m) and Hoho in + KYORAN J1 J2 AS = 278 /
347. Bankai: KYORAN J1-J4 AS 240 / 300 (self -160); ZANKAI J1-J4 AS 319 / 396 (self -160), NS a-d SA J2-J4 AS **461 / 577 (self -320)**;
the way back CHIGIRI 247 / 309, TATE-GOTO 288 / 358 (self -40, -1 Konpaku). Every listed route is a true combo (gaps 0) except the
meaty loop (gap 3: R13, R14).

**The triangle at distance (T)**: at 1.5-2.5 m my J1 beats his J1 (S9 vs S14) and my NS1a beats his NS1a (S14 vs S22 / S20); J vs NS
cancels (the exchange decides). At 3.5 m only the Shikai reaches (J1 and the lunge beat both his J1 and NS1a); Ichigo's NS1a hits the
sealed form there. KESSA's J1 (S8) beats my sealed J1.

**The exchange (X, Y, Z)**: as the J side (my J1 cancelled his NS1a) the AS beats his NS going on and any SA of his that started before
it, and loses to any SA started after it, until the AS's hit (the later start wins, DUEL_CORE §2.1.1): Y's grid shows the boundary moving
with my AS's delay (+0: his SA on f12-26 wins; +6: f18-32; +10: f22-34). No delay wins: against his HARD CPU the AS lands first in 3 of 10
seeds at +0, +6 and +10 against Ichigo (he SAs in 7) and 3 / 4 / 0 of 10 against Hisagi (at +10 his NS lands first). The X rows' "AS 6-10
frames late beats his SA at f14" is only that boundary. 14 frames late his NS lands; J2 always loses. As the NS side my SA beats his J2 and
ties or loses to his AS; NS1b loses to his AS (the sealed NS1b beats his J2).

**Whiff punishes (W, against his real free tick from F)**: his J1 whiff at 2.6 m (free on tick 47; KESSA's 41) is punished by any of my
five openers pressed up to f30, the latest tried (KESSA: f22; my J1 still at f30); his NS1a / NS3a / bind whiffs (3.6-6.5 m) only by the
Shikai lunge (pressed f22-46) and the KYORAN lunge (f30-46; never the bind); Hisagi's NS2a whiff at 4.2 m slides him 1 m in, so the sealed
NS1a, the Shikai J1 and both lunges punish it (f22-30). Otherwise sealed Kenpachi cannot whiff-punish anything that whiffs beyond 3.3 m.
Hisagi's dead-zone NS1a at 2.4 m is punished by everything (f22-44; the NS1a and the lunges from f14).

**My whiffs (S, against my free tick read from the log: J1 42, sealed NS1a 60, Shikai lunge 64)**: my J1 whiffs are never punished by
his J1 (out of his reach after my 0.6 m slide); Ichigo's NS1a and rush and Hisagi's bind thrown on f10-18 punish them; my sealed NS1a
whiff at 3.8 m is punished by Ichigo's NS1a (f10-26) and rush (f18-26) and by Hisagi's bind (f10-26); a whiffed Shikai lunge (6.2 m) by
Ichigo's rush (f18-26) and Hisagi's NS1a and bind (f10-26).

**Approach (A)**: his walk-in from 4.5 m runs into my sealed NS1a (any timing f6-24) and J1 (f18+); his Step-in + J1 loses to my J1
pressed f0-16 (the Step's iframes 3-9 end before my hit) and cancels / beats my NS1a; his Hoho perfect-counters my sealed J1 (Hoho f0-6),
NS1a (f3-9), Shikai lunge (f6-9); my Hoho counters his J1 (f6-10) and his NS1a (f14-18, f10 on Hisagi).

**Wake-up (K)**: after AS1's knockdown his first free tick is 135; the meaty J1 (free+28, hits on 137) beats his buffered wake-up J and
back Step, cancels his wake-up NS1a (my string then lands, 100 / 126); **only a Hoho on his first free tick (134-135) beats it (perfect
counter)**; at tick 136 the meaty already hits. The late J1 (free+40) cancels with his buffered J1 and loses to a Hoho at 140.

**Ranged (G)**: vs the Getsuga route at 5 m: Hoho f0 / Step in / walk 24, then NS1 a b c, hits his charge (76 / 96); the Shikai lunge f0
hits at 5 m and after a Step from 6.6 m; KYORAN's lunge (4.9 m) eats the wave; a J of any form at f48-60 cancels the wave (nothing lands).
vs Hisagi's NS1a / bind at 4-4.5 m: the Shikai lunge (S16) and KYORAN lunge (S14) land first; a sealed Step in + J1 beats his NS1a and
ranged J1 (not the bind); a Hoho on f8 perfect-counters the bind, the ranged J1 and the bind-mode NS1a; the sealed NS1a and walk-in lose.

**Kikon (C)**: red victim: sealed ptr 1 -1, ptr 2 **-3**; ptr 3 -1; Shikai INSEKI-GIRI -2; ZANKAI TATE-GOTO -3 (and -1 to Kenpachi).
A non-red Ichigo or Hisagi cancels the follow-up with J1 (0 Konpaku).

| Exchange | I | H | IK | Hr | Hb |
